# Package net.thevpc.nuts.text

<a id="pkg-net-thevpc-nuts-text"></a>

Structured text, messages, formatting, rendering, and object writers.



`NText` forms a tree of plain, styled, linked, titled, list, table, and
code nodes. Formatters, parsers, transformers, visitors, themes, terminal commands,
and renderers turn that tree or arbitrary objects into human- or machine-readable
output. The package also contains message and internationalization abstractions.

```java
public enum NContentType implements NEnum
```

**Author:** thevpc

| :--- | :--- |

| `XML` | xml format |
| `PROPS` | java properties |
| `TABLE` | (terminal/ascii) table format |
| `TREE` | (terminal/ascii) tree format |
| `PLAIN` | plain (no) format |

| `YAML` | YAML Ain't Markup Language |

<a id="m-NText-filteredText"></a>

#### filteredText

```java
String filteredText()
```

this method removes all special "nuts print format" sequences support
and returns the raw string to be printed on an
ordinary [PrintStream](#cls-PrintStream)

**Returns:**

string without any escape sequences so that the text printed
correctly on any non formatted [PrintStream](#cls-PrintStream)

<a id="m-NTextBuilder-flatten"></a>

#### flatten

```java
NTextBuilder flatten()
```

replaces the builder content with the simplest text in the form of suite
of plain or styled text elements. the possible returned types are plain
text (NutsTextPlain) if there is no styling or styled plain
(NutsTextStyled) if any style is detected.



Compound nodes are flattened so than the returned instance is one of the
following: - a single line plain text (plain text than either does not
include any newline or is a single newline) - a styled plain (style nodes
that have a single line plain text child)

**Returns:**

`this` instance with flattened children

<a id="m-NTextTheme-of"></a>

#### of

```java
@NGetter
static NTextTheme of()
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

Creates a new instance.

**Returns:**

of result



---

<a id="m-NTextTheme-of"></a>

#### of

```java
@NGetter
static NOptional<NTextTheme> of(String name)
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

resolve theme by name or path.

when a simple name, it is read from classpath:/META-INF/ntf-themes/*.ntf-theme.
Themes loaded by name are cached.

when a path (contains separators), it is loaded using NPath
Themes loaded by path are not necessarily cached (do not rely on that).

when no name or path is passed (null or blank), the default theme is loaded.

the default theme can be configured using --theme in nuts bootstrapping. When none is configured,
the implementation will create one of its own. That one can even (and usually is) OS dependent.

**Parameters:**

- `name` - theme name or path

**Returns:**

optional of a theme



---

<a id="m-NTextTheme-of"></a>

#### of

```java
@NGetter
static NOptional<NTextTheme> of(NPath path)
```

**Accessor:** Property getter.

resolve theme from path.
when null is passed an empty optional is returned.

**Parameters:**

- `path` - 

**Returns:**

optional of the loaded theme

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-text-NTextTransformConfig"></a>

## NTextTransformConfig

```java
public class NTextTransformConfig implements Cloneable, NBlankable
```

### Fields

| Modifier and Type | Field | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `private boolean` | `filtered` | when true all styles are removed, transform any node to PLAIN, or LIST only |
| `private boolean` | `flatten` | when true, every node is split over newlines. |
| `private boolean` | `normalize` | when true, transform any node to PLAIN, STYLED or LIST (to basic) |
| `private boolean` | `applyTheme` | when true all styles are transformed to plain colors |
| `private boolean` | `basicTrueStyles` |  |
| `private boolean` | `processTitleNumbers` | when true any title will be prefixed with an incremental number according to titleNumberSequence |
| `private NTitleSequence` | `titleNumberSequence` | sequence to use when processTitleNumbers==true |
| `private boolean` | `processIncludes` | when true, include commands are processed, resources are loaded to replace the given command |
| `private NPath` | `currentDir` | current directory to load from included files |
| `private boolean` | `processVars` | when true, replace all "${varName" } literal by evaluating varProvider.apply("${varName") }. |
| `private Function&lt;String,String&gt;` | `varProvider` |  |
| `private String` | `anchor` |  |
| `private Integer` | `rootLevel` | when provided, ensure that to root level of the document is translated to rootLevel |
| `private String` | `themeName` |  |
| `private ClassLoader` | `importClassLoader` | when provided, try to use this class loader for 'classpath:' resources |

<a id="cls-net-thevpc-nuts-text-NTreeObjectWriter"></a>

## NTreeObjectWriter

```java
public interface NTreeObjectWriter extends NContentTypeWriter
```

Tree Format handles terminal output in Tree format. It's one of the many
formats supported bu nuts such as plain,table, xml, json. To use Tree format,
given an instance ws of Nuts Workspace you can :
```java
ws.
```

**Since:** 0.5.5
